I didn’t see Victor doing anything strange, but Tara’s whispering raises a red flag. Let’s dig deeper into Tara’s behavior and see if others noticed anything else.

  Noah: I think we should consider who had the opportunity to kill Mia. Since Mia was the Seer, the werewolf would have wanted to eliminate her to avoid being exposed. Let's look at who was awake or had the chance to act during the night. I didn't see Victor doing anything suspicious, but I did notice Tara whispering to someone earlier. Maybe we should investigate Tara's actions more closely.
  Victor: I think we need to focus on who had the opportunity to kill Mia. Since the werewolf would target the Seer first, we should look at who was active or had the chance to act during the night. I didn’t see Victor doing anything strange, but Tara’s whispering raises a red flag. Let’s dig deeper into Tara’s behavior and see if others noticed anything else.
  Carol: I think we're all looking in the wrong direction. Mia was eliminated by the werewolf, but we need to think logically—who had the chance to act? I didn’t see anyone suspicious last night, but Tara’s whispering is worth looking into. However, I believe we should focus on who benefits most from Mia’s death. The werewolf would want to eliminate the Seer, but they also need to avoid being voted out. Let’s not forget that we’re all under pressure, and pointing fingers without proof could lead us astra
  Kate: I think we need to focus on who had the actual opportunity to kill Mia. As the Doctor, I was awake during the night and saw Tara whispering to someone—maybe that’s a clue. However, I also noticed that Victor was acting strangely when Mia was eliminated. Let’s not just focus on whispers but on who had the chance to act. I’m leaning toward Victor being a suspect.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (7): Grace, Eve, Noah, Victor, Carol, Kate, Tara

  Day 1: Mia (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Grace: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf. Since Mia, the Seer, was eliminated, we don't have any more information from her. I noticed that Victor was acting strangely last night—maybe he's hiding something. Let's talk about his behavior and see if others noticed anything.
  Eve: I think Victor's behavior is suspicious, but I also noticed Carol acting oddly during the night. As a werewolf, I know we need to be careful who we point fingers at. Let's not jump to conclusions—maybe we should focus on who had the chance to kill Mia.
  Noah: I think we should consider who had the opportunity to kill Mia. Since Mia was the Seer, the werewolf would have wanted to eliminate her to avoid being exposed. Let's look at who was awake or had the chance to act during the night. I didn't see Victor doing anything suspicious, but I did notice Tara whispering to someone earlier. Maybe we should investigate Tara's actions more closely.
  Victor: I think we need to focus on who had the opportunity to kill Mia. Since the werewolf would target the Seer first, we should look at who was active or had the chance to act during the night. I didn’t see Victor doing anything strange, but Tara’s whispering raises a red flag. Let’s dig deeper into Tara’s behavior and see if others noticed anything else.
  Carol: I think we're all looking in the wrong direction. Mia was eliminated by the werewolf, but we need to think logically—who had the chance to act? I didn’t see anyone suspicious last night, but Tara’s whispering is worth looking into. However, I believe we should focus on who benefits most from Mia’s death. The werewolf would want to eliminate the Seer, but they also need to avoid being voted out. Let’s not forget that we’re all under pressure, and pointing fingers without proof could lead us astra
  Kate: I think we need to focus on who had the actual opportunity to kill Mia. As the Doctor, I was awake during the night and saw Tara whispering to someone—maybe that’s a clue. However, I also noticed that Victor was acting strangely when Mia was eliminated. Let’s not just focus on whispers but on who had the chance to act. I’m leaning toward Victor being a suspect.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who had the actual opportunity to kill Mia. As the Doctor, I was awake during the night and saw Tara whispering to someone—maybe that’s a clue. However, I also noticed that Victor was acting strangely when Mia was eliminated. Let’s not just focus on whispers but on who had the chance to act. I’m leaning toward Victor being a suspect.
